# Compute, power and orbit: the documented build-out of AI compute, 2023–2026

Generated from `pack.json` by `node research/compute-power-orbit/tools.mjs render`; do not edit by hand. Candidate research pack outside application data. Machine-prepared and audited by the same model family; not editorially accepted; no human review; cross-family check not yet run. Check date 2026-10-28. Method and limits: [README](README.md) and [scope](scope.md).

Each record states what one source says, dated by the kind of date the source gives. An announcement is recorded as announced: not as a signed contract, a built site, delivered power or shipped chips. Figures are as stated, with their basis, and are never summed across sources. A target date is a projection, listed apart from events. An argument is a proposition attributed to the text that states it, never a fact.

## Data-centre projects

### Abilene, Texas (Crusoe campuses)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Crusoe stated that with the second phase its Abilene facility would total eight buildings and a total power capacity of 1.2 gigawatts.** `cpo-dc-crusoe-abilene-1-2gw`
  2025-03-18 · announced · US · `plans_capacity`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 1.2 GW (as worded: "a total power capacity of 1.2 gigawatts (GW)") (measures: facility power (stated as total power capacity); the release also mentions a 1.2 GW grid interconnection); stated target completion: 2026 (stated by Crusoe)
  _Limits:_ Planned total power capacity of the campus; the release separately refers to "the 1.2 GW grid interconnection" in Lancium's power plan. Neither figure is IT load or compute delivered. Crusoe states construction of the second phase had begun and expected completion in mid-2026, a target; its 27 March 2026 release gives completion of the second phase "by the end of 2026".

  > “bringing the total facility to eight buildings, approximately 4 million square feet, and a total power capacity of 1.2 gigawatts (GW)”  
  > [Crusoe expands AI data center campus in Abilene to 1.2 gigawatts](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-expands-ai-data-center-campus-in-abilene-to-1-2-gigawatts), paragraph 1
  > “The second phase of construction, expected to be completed in mid-2026”  
  > [Crusoe expands AI data center campus in Abilene to 1.2 gigawatts](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-expands-ai-data-center-campus-in-abilene-to-1-2-gigawatts), paragraph 1
  > “In addition to the 1.2 GW grid interconnection”  
  > [Crusoe expands AI data center campus in Abilene to 1.2 gigawatts](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-expands-ai-data-center-campus-in-abilene-to-1-2-gigawatts), paragraph 6 · context
  > “is expected to reach completion by the end of 2026”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), section "Delivering gigascale AI infrastructure", paragraph 1 · context

- **Crusoe stated that the new campus brings the total projected capacity across the full Abilene site to 2.1 gigawatts.** `cpo-dc-crusoe-abilene-total-2-1gw`
  2026-03-27 · announced · US · `plans_capacity`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 2.1 GW (as worded: "total projected capacity across the full Abilene site") (measures: projected capacity (basis not stated))
  _Limits:_ Crusoe's own projected aggregate for its Abilene site; not built capacity and not a figure for either campus.

  > “bringing the total projected capacity across the full Abilene site to 2.1 gigawatts (GW)”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1

- **Crusoe announced on 27 March 2026 a new 900 MW AI factory campus in Abilene, adjacent to its existing campus, to support large-scale AI workloads for Microsoft, with land clearing underway and the first building expected to be energized in mid-2027.** `cpo-dc-crusoe-microsoft-campus-announced`
  2026-03-27 · announced · US · proposed predicate `announced_site`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 900 MW (as worded: "the 900 MW site") (measures: site capacity (basis not stated; the onsite power plant is also stated as 900MW)); stage: announced; stated target operation: 2027-mid (stated by Crusoe)
  _Limits:_ An announced development, with site preparation stated as underway; no contract terms with Microsoft are stated beyond the support relationship. The 900 MW is also the stated size of the on-site power plant, and each of the two buildings is stated as designed for 336 MW of critical IT load (design figures, not operating capacity).
  > “Supporting large scale AI workloads for Microsoft, the 900 MW site includes two new buildings and an onsite power plant”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1
  > “with the first building expected to be energized in mid-2027”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1
  > the new campus is located adjacent to Crusoe's existing Abilene AI factory infrastructure, and land clearing and site preparation are underway. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1
  > the release describes a 900MW on-site power plant and each of the two new buildings as designed to deliver 336 MW of critical IT load. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), section "Energy-first AI infrastructure" · context

#### Stated status

- **Crusoe stated in March 2025 that it began constructing its Abilene AI data center in June 2024 and that the initial phase, two buildings and 200+ megawatts, was expected to be energized in the first half of 2025.** `cpo-dc-crusoe-abilene-initial-phase`
  2025-03-18 · announced · US · proposed predicate `site_status_stated`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity more than 200 MW (as worded: "200+ megawatts") (measures: not stated); stated construction start stated: 2024-06 (stated by Crusoe); stated target operation: 2025-H1 (stated by Crusoe)
  _Limits:_ Construction start as stated by Crusoe in March 2025 (not a contemporaneous record); energization in 2025 is a target in this release. Crusoe does not name Stargate, OpenAI or Oracle; whether this campus is the dataset's Stargate I is not stated in Crusoe's texts read here.

  > “Crusoe began constructing the AI data center in Abilene in June 2024.”  
  > [Crusoe expands AI data center campus in Abilene to 1.2 gigawatts](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-expands-ai-data-center-campus-in-abilene-to-1-2-gigawatts), paragraph 2
  > “comprising two buildings at 980,000 square feet and 200+ megawatts, is expected to be energized in the first half of 2025”  
  > [Crusoe expands AI data center campus in Abilene to 1.2 gigawatts](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-expands-ai-data-center-campus-in-abilene-to-1-2-gigawatts), paragraph 2

- **Crusoe stated on 27 March 2026 that the first two buildings of its Abilene campus had been energized and that the second phase was expected to reach completion by the end of 2026.** `cpo-dc-crusoe-abilene-energized-2026`
  2026-03-27 · stated operating · US · proposed predicate `site_status_stated`
  _Date:_ Dated by the statement; the release does not give the date of energization.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stated target completion: 2026-12 (stated by Crusoe)
  _Limits:_ Energization of two buildings as stated by Crusoe, not a statement that the whole campus or the second phase operates. The completion target differs from the March 2025 release's "mid-2026".
  > “energized the first two buildings of the Abilene campus in under one year”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1
  > “is expected to reach completion by the end of 2026”  
  > [Crusoe Announces New 900 MW AI Factory Campus in Abilene, Texas to Support Microsoft AI Infrastructure](https://www.crusoe.ai/resources/newsroom/crusoe-announces-new-900-mw-ai-factory-campus-in-abilene-texas-to-support-microsoft-ai-infrastructure), section "Delivering gigascale AI infrastructure", paragraph 1

### Amazon and AWS: stated AI data-centre investments

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Amazon announced on 9 June 2025 plans to invest at least $20 billion in Pennsylvania to expand its data center infrastructure to support AI and cloud computing, naming Salem Township and Falls Township as the first communities identified as sites.** `cpo-dc-amazon-pennsylvania-20bn`
  2025-06-09 · announced · US · `plans_infrastructure_spend`
  _Date:_ Date from the page metadata.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount at least 20,000,000,000 USD (transaction)
  _Limits:_ A stated plan; it covers several campuses and does not separate AI from cloud capacity. No capacity or schedule is stated for either named township; separate site entities are not created in this pass.

  > “Amazon today announced plans to invest at least $20 billion in Pennsylvania to expand our data center infrastructure to support artificial intelligence (AI) and cloud computing technologies.”  
  > [Amazon plans to invest $20 billion in Pennsylvania to expand cloud computing infrastructure and advance AI innovation](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/aws/amazon-pennsylvania-investment-cloud-infrastructure-ai-innovation), article paragraph 1
  > “Salem Township and Falls Township are the first communities identified as sites for these future innovation campuses.”  
  > [Amazon plans to invest $20 billion in Pennsylvania to expand cloud computing infrastructure and advance AI innovation](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/aws/amazon-pennsylvania-investment-cloud-infrastructure-ai-innovation), article paragraph 1

- **Amazon stated that the investment will add nearly 1.3 gigawatts of AI and supercomputing capacity across the AWS Top Secret, AWS Secret and AWS GovCloud (US) Regions.** `cpo-dc-aws-gov-1-3gw`
  2025-11-24 · announced · US · `plans_capacity`
  _Date:_ Date from the page metadata (datePublished 2025-11-24); the page text shows no date.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity approximate 1.3 GW (as worded: "nearly 1.3 gigawatts of AI and supercomputing capacity") (measures: not stated)
  _Limits:_ Planned capacity across several regions; the basis of the gigawatts is not stated and no site-level figure is given.

  > “will add nearly 1.3 gigawatts of AI and supercomputing capacity across AWS Top Secret, AWS Secret, and AWS GovCloud (US) Regions”  
  > [Amazon to invest up to $50 billion to expand AI and supercomputing infrastructure for US government agencies](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/company-news/amazon-ai-investment-us-federal-agencies), paragraph 1

- **Amazon announced on 24 November 2025 an investment of up to $50 billion to expand AI and supercomputing capabilities for AWS US government customers, set to break ground in 2026.** `cpo-dc-aws-gov-50bn`
  2025-11-24 · announced · US · `plans_infrastructure_spend`
  _Date:_ Date from the page metadata; see source version note.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount up to 50,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stated construction start target: 2026 (stated by Amazon)
  _Limits:_ An "up to" plan; groundbreaking in 2026 is a target, and the stated addition of nearly 1.3 gigawatts of AI and supercomputing capacity is a planned figure, not built capacity. No site is named.
  > “Today, Amazon announced an investment of up to $50 billion to expand AI and supercomputing capabilities for Amazon Web Services (AWS) U.S. government customers.”  
  > [Amazon to invest up to $50 billion to expand AI and supercomputing infrastructure for US government agencies](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/company-news/amazon-ai-investment-us-federal-agencies), paragraph 1
  > “This investment, set to break ground in 2026, will add nearly 1.3 gigawatts of AI and supercomputing capacity”  
  > [Amazon to invest up to $50 billion to expand AI and supercomputing infrastructure for US government agencies](https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/company-news/amazon-ai-investment-us-federal-agencies), paragraph 1

### Anthropic: US data centres with Fluidstack

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Anthropic announced on 12 November 2025 a $50 billion investment in American computing infrastructure, building data centers with Fluidstack in Texas and New York, with sites coming online throughout 2026.** `cpo-dc-anthropic-50bn`
  2025-11-12 · announced · US · `plans_infrastructure_spend`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount 50,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stated target operation: 2026 (stated by Anthropic)
  _Limits:_ An announced investment and a target for sites coming online; no capacity, no individual site and no contract terms with Fluidstack are stated.
  > “a $50 billion investment in American computing infrastructure, building data centers with Fluidstack in Texas and New York”  
  > [Anthropic invests $50 billion in American AI infrastructure](https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-invests-50-billion-in-american-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 1
  > “sites coming online throughout 2026”  
  > [Anthropic invests $50 billion in American AI infrastructure](https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-invests-50-billion-in-american-ai-infrastructure), paragraph 2

### EU AI gigafactories (InvestAI)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **EuroHPC JU states that 76 expressions of interest proposing AI gigafactories in 16 Member States across 60 sites were submitted to the call that closed on 20 June 2025.** `cpo-dc-eurohpc-aigf-76-eoi`
  2026-10-28 · snapshot · EU · proposed predicate `programme_status_stated`
  _Date:_ Undated page as served on the check date; the call closed on 20 June 2025.
  _Limits:_ Expressions of interest are not applications, selections or awards. The Commission's own news item on the count could not be retrieved.
  > “A total of 76 expressions of interest proposing to set up AI Gigafactories in 16 Member States across 60 different sites have been submitted”  
  > [AI Gigafactories Consultations](https://www.eurohpc-ju.europa.eu:443/ai-gigafactories/ai-gigafactories-consultations_en), paragraph 2

#### Issue and status of texts

- **The European Commission announced InvestAI at the AI Action Summit in Paris on 11 February 2025, an initiative to mobilise €200 billion for investment in AI including a new European fund of €20 billion for AI gigafactories.** `cpo-dc-eu-investai-published`
  2025-02-11 · published · EU · `published`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount 20,000,000,000 EUR (transaction)
  _Limits:_ An announced initiative; the €200 billion is an amount to be mobilised, not committed public funds. Initial Commission funding is stated to come from existing EU programmes.
  > “Today, at the Artificial Intelligence (AI) Action Summit in Paris, Commission President Ursula von der Leyen has launched InvestAI”  
  > [EU launches InvestAI initiative to mobilise €200 billion of investment in artificial intelligence (IP/25/467)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/25/467&language=en), paragraph 1
  > the initiative is to mobilise EUR 200 billion for investment in AI, including a new European fund of EUR 20 billion for AI gigafactories. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [EU launches InvestAI initiative to mobilise €200 billion of investment in artificial intelligence (IP/25/467)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/25/467&language=en), paragraph 1
  > the Commission's initial funding for InvestAI will come from existing EU funding programmes with a digital component (Digital Europe Programme, Horizon Europe and InvestEU). _(paraphrase)_  
  > [EU launches InvestAI initiative to mobilise €200 billion of investment in artificial intelligence (IP/25/467)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/25/467&language=en), paragraph on funding · context

#### What the text states

- **The InvestAI announcement states that the fund will finance four future AI gigafactories, and that the gigafactories will have around 100,000 latest-generation AI chips.** `cpo-dc-eu-investai-four-gigafactories`
  2025-02-11 · published · EU · `states_provision`
  _Date:_ Date of the press release; the page is marked as updated on 10/04/2025 and the text read is the updated version.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity approximate 100000 chips (as worded: "The gigafactories will have around 100 000 latest-generation AI chips"; whether per gigafactory is not stated, though the release compares the figure with the AI factories) (measures: chips)
  _Limits:_ A stated intention of the initiative; no gigafactory site is named, and chip counts are planned characteristics, not installations. The press release is marked "Updated on 10/04/2025"; the text read is that version.
  > “The EU's InvestAI fund will finance four future AI gigafactories across the EU.”  
  > [EU launches InvestAI initiative to mobilise €200 billion of investment in artificial intelligence (IP/25/467)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/25/467&language=en), paragraph 4
  > “The gigafactories will have around 100 000 latest-generation AI chips”  
  > [EU launches InvestAI initiative to mobilise €200 billion of investment in artificial intelligence (IP/25/467)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/25/467&language=en), paragraph 4

  > [关于印发《数据中心绿色低碳发展专项行动计划》的通知 (发改环资〔2024〕970号)](https://www.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengceku/202407/content_6964165.htm), metadata block

- **The NDRC, NEA, MIIT and National Data Administration issued the Action Plan on Promoting Two-Way Empowerment of Artificial Intelligence and Energy (国能发科技〔2026〕34号), dated 8 April 2026 and published on 8 May 2026.** `cpo-pw-cn-ai-energy-plan-issued`
  2026-04-08 · issued · China · `published`
  _Date:_ Date under the signatures in the reprint; 发布日期 2026-05-08.
  _Limits:_ Records issuance only. Evidence is a provincial-government reprint and the National Data Administration's republication of the NEA announcement, not the NEA's own page.

  > “国家数据局 2026年4月8日” — _working translation:_ “Selekoa working translation: National Data Administration, 8 April 2026”

  > The National Energy Administration, with the NDRC, MIIT and National Data Administration, issued the plan, which sets 29 key tasks including securing energy supply for computing facilities and computing–power coordination. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [《关于促进人工智能与能源双向赋能的行动方案》印发](https://www.nda.gov.cn/sjj/zwgk/zcfb/0508/20260508194112925527578_pc.html), paragraphs 1–2

#### What the text states

- **Task (4) of the 2024 data-centre plan sets the goal that by the end of 2025 a two-way computing–electricity coordination mechanism is initially formed and green electricity exceeds 80% for new data centres at national hub nodes, and encourages co-location of new data centres with renewable generation and pilots of direct green-power supply.** `cpo-pw-cn-dc-plan-green-power-2025`
  2024-07-03 · issued · China · `states_provision`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity more than 80 % green electricity (as worded: 超过 80%) (measures: share of electricity, new hub-node data centres); stated target stated: 2025 (stated by National Development and Reform Commission (国家发展和改革委员会))
  _Limits:_ Targets set by the plan; whether they were met is not recorded. 'National hub nodes' are the hubs of the national integrated computing network.

  > “到 2025 年底，算力电力双向协同机制初步形成，国家枢纽节点新建数据中心绿电占比超过 80%” — _working translation:_ “Selekoa working translation: By the end of 2025, a two-way computing–electricity coordination mechanism will be initially formed, and green electricity will exceed 80% for new data centres at national hub nodes”  
  > [数据中心绿色低碳发展专项行动计划 (Special Action Plan for the Green and Low-Carbon Development of Data Centres), attachment to 发改环资〔2024〕970号](https://www.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengceku/202407/P020240724272072479001.pdf), 二、（四）提升可再生能源利用水平, PDF p. 3
  > “引导新建数据中心与可再生能源发电等协同布局” — _working translation:_ “Selekoa working translation: guide new data centres to be sited in coordination with renewable generation and the like”  
  > [数据中心绿色低碳发展专项行动计划 (Special Action Plan for the Green and Low-Carbon Development of Data Centres), attachment to 发改环资〔2024〕970号](https://www.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengceku/202407/P020240724272072479001.pdf), 二、（四）, PDF p. 3
  > “鼓励有关地区探索开展数据中心绿电直供” — _working translation:_ “Selekoa working translation: encourage the regions concerned to explore direct green-power supply to data centres”  
  > [数据中心绿色低碳发展专项行动计划 (Special Action Plan for the Green and Low-Carbon Development of Data Centres), attachment to 发改环资〔2024〕970号](https://www.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengceku/202407/P020240724272072479001.pdf), 二、（四）, PDF p. 3

- **Task (1) of the 2026 AI–energy plan calls for exploring, in the light of regional energy and water carrying capacity, co-construction of million-kilowatt-scale AI computing facilities with matching energy systems, with pilots in suitable regions, to advance integrated computing–power coordination (算电协同).** `cpo-pw-cn-ai-energy-plan-gw-pilots`
  2026-04-08 · issued · China · `states_provision`
  _Limits:_ An exploratory task; no pilot is recorded. 百万千瓦级 (million-kilowatt scale) is about 1 GW (Selekoa's conversion); the plan does not say whether it refers to facility load or generation. The task is conditioned on regional energy and water carrying capacity. Text read from a reprint on the Heilongjiang government portal (published there 8 May 2026); the plan was issued by NDRC, NEA, MIIT and NDA as 国能发科技〔2026〕34号, dated 8 April 2026 under the signatures.
  > “结合地区能源、水资源等承载力，探索百万千瓦级人工智能算力设施与配套能源系统协同建设，选择具备条件的地区开展试点，推动算电协同一体化发展” — _working translation:_ “Selekoa working translation: taking account of regional energy, water and other carrying capacity, explore coordinated construction of million-kilowatt-scale AI computing facilities and matching energy systems, select qualified regions for pilots, and promote integrated development of computing–power coordination”  
  > [国家发展改革委 国家能源局 工业和信息化部 国家数据局印发《关于促进人工智能与能源双向赋能的行动方案》的通知 (国能发科技〔2026〕34号)](https://www.hlj.gov.cn/hljzqc/c100116/202605/c00_31938857.shtml), 二、（一）统筹优化能源资源与算力布局

### Crane Clean Energy Center (Three Mile Island Unit 1) restart

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Constellation announced on 20 September 2024 that it had signed a 20-year power purchase agreement with Microsoft that would pave the way for restarting Three Mile Island Unit 1 as the Crane Clean Energy Center, with Microsoft purchasing energy from the plant as part of its goal to match the power its PJM data centers use with carbon-free energy.** `cpo-pw-msft-constellation-crane-ppa`
  2024-09-20 · announced · US · proposed predicate `power_agreement`
  _Date:_ Signing stated by Constellation in the release; the signature date itself is not given.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity approximate 835 megawatts (as worded: 'approximately 835 megawatts of carbon-free energy to the grid') (measures: generation added to the grid); stage: contracted; power: nuclear, restart; stated target operation: 2028 (stated by Constellation Energy Corporation)
  _Limits:_ Constellation's announcement of a signed agreement; the contract terms and price are not published. The restart required NRC approval (see cpo-pw-crane-nrc-fuel-amendment) and is not recorded as having occurred. The 835 MW figure is Constellation's wording for energy added to the grid, not a contracted delivery volume; the 2028 online date is a 2024 projection, later restated by Constellation as 2027 (cpo-pw-crane-nrc-fuel-amendment).

  > “announced today the signing of a 20-year power purchase agreement with Microsoft that will pave the way for the launch of the Crane Clean Energy Center (CCEC) and restart of Three Mile Island Unit 1”  
  > [Constellation to Launch Crane Clean Energy Center, Restoring Jobs and Carbon-Free Power to The Grid](https://www.constellationenergy.com/news/2024/Constellation-to-Launch-Crane-Clean-Energy-Center-Restoring-Jobs-and-Carbon-Free-Power-to-The-Grid.html), paragraph beginning “LONDONDERRY, PA (Sept 20, 2024) Constellation…”
  > “Microsoft will purchase energy from the renewed plant as part of its goal to help match the power its data centers in PJM use with carbon-free energy.”  
  > [Constellation to Launch Crane Clean Energy Center, Restoring Jobs and Carbon-Free Power to The Grid](https://www.constellationenergy.com/news/2024/Constellation-to-Launch-Crane-Clean-Energy-Center-Restoring-Jobs-and-Carbon-Free-Power-to-The-Grid.html), paragraph beginning “LONDONDERRY, PA (Sept 20, 2024) Constellation…”
  > “add approximately 835 megawatts of carbon-free energy to the grid”  
  > [Constellation to Launch Crane Clean Energy Center, Restoring Jobs and Carbon-Free Power to The Grid](https://www.constellationenergy.com/news/2024/Constellation-to-Launch-Crane-Clean-Energy-Center-Restoring-Jobs-and-Carbon-Free-Power-to-The-Grid.html), subheading
  > “The CCEC is expected to be online in 2028.”  
  > [Constellation to Launch Crane Clean Energy Center, Restoring Jobs and Carbon-Free Power to The Grid](https://www.constellationenergy.com/news/2024/Constellation-to-Launch-Crane-Clean-Energy-Center-Restoring-Jobs-and-Carbon-Free-Power-to-The-Grid.html), paragraph beginning “The Unit 1 reactor is located…”

- **Constellation stated on 6 August 2026 that the NRC had approved a fuel licence amendment request for the Crane Clean Energy Center, and that it was moving closer to restarting operations in 2027.** `cpo-pw-crane-nrc-fuel-amendment`
  2026-08-06 · announced · US · proposed predicate `regulator_decision`
  _Date:_ Date of Constellation's statement; the date of the NRC action is not given in the release.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ power: nuclear, restart; stated target operation: 2027 (stated by Constellation Energy Corporation)
  _Limits:_ Rests on Constellation's report; the NRC approval document was not retrieved. A fuel licence amendment is one step; Constellation does not state that the plant operates. 2027 is a target that replaces the 2028 projection of September 2024.
  > “the NRC has approved a fuel license amendment request for the Crane Clean Energy Center — a major milestone moving us closer to restarting operations in 2027”  
  > [Constellation Reports Second Quarter 2026 Results](https://www.constellationenergy.com/news/2026/08/constellation-reports-second-quarter-2026-results.html), Recent Developments and Second Quarter Highlights, first item

### EU energy measures for data centres

- **The Commission announced on 3 June 2026, as part of its tech sovereignty package, a Strategic Roadmap for Digitalisation and AI in the Energy Sector, stating that growth of digital infrastructure is expected to increase electricity demand and that the roadmap will ensure data centres are integrated into the energy system in a sustainable and transparent manner.** `cpo-pw-eu-ai-energy-roadmap`
  2026-06-03 · announced · EU · proposed predicate `announced_plan`
  _Limits:_ Rests on the press release; the roadmap text was not retrieved (digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu refuses). A roadmap is a non-legislative plan. The Cloud and AI Development Act proposed the same day, with its aim to triple EU data-centre capacity in five to seven years, is not recorded here.
  > the package includes the Chips Act 2.0 and Cloud and AI Development Act proposals, the Open Source Strategy and a Strategic Roadmap for Digitalisation and AI in Energy. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Commission proposes tech sovereignty package to strengthen Europe's digital autonomy and resilience (IP/26/1187)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/26/1187&language=en), opening section
  > “the growth of digital infrastructure across our continent is expected to increase demand for electricity”  
  > [Commission proposes tech sovereignty package to strengthen Europe's digital autonomy and resilience (IP/26/1187)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/26/1187&language=en), section 'Digitalising Europe's energy system while ensuring sustainable digitalisation'
  > “This roadmap will ensure that data centres are integrated into our energy system in a sustainable and transparent manner.”  
  > [Commission proposes tech sovereignty package to strengthen Europe's digital autonomy and resilience (IP/26/1187)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/26/1187&language=en), same section
  > the Cloud and AI Development Act aims to triple data centre capacity in Europe over the next five to seven years. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Commission proposes tech sovereignty package to strengthen Europe's digital autonomy and resilience (IP/26/1187)](https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/documents?reference=IP/26/1187&language=en), section "Building Europe's cloud and AI capacity" · context

- **Google announced on 30 June 2025 an offtake agreement to take 200 MW of carbon-free energy from Commonwealth Fusion Systems' first commercial plant, ARC, to be developed in Chesterfield, Virginia, together with a second capital investment in CFS and an option to buy power from future plants.** `cpo-pw-google-cfs-offtake`
  2025-06-30 · announced · US · proposed predicate `power_agreement`

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

  _Limits:_ An announced agreement for future reactors; no reactor named in it operated at the check date as far as these sources show. 500 MW is an upper bound for power to grids, not a contracted delivery to Google. Reported terms (minimum commitments ramping in 120 MW steps from 2025, a one-time option to cap at 480 MW) are the reporter's account of Talen's disclosure. The sale price is not recorded here. ## Chip supply

### Chinese export controls bearing on chips (2023–2025)

#### Issue and status of texts

- **The Ministry of Commerce and the General Administration of Customs issued joint Announcement No. 23 of 2023, dated 3 July 2023, imposing export controls on gallium- and germanium-related items with effect from 1 August 2023.** `cpo-ch-cn-ga-ge-2023-issued`
  2023-07-03 · issued · China · `enacted`
  _Date:_ Effective 1 August 2023 (Article 8).
  _Limits:_ The announcement controls materials; its text does not mention chips or AI, and their relevance to chip supply is context, not a statement of the text.

  > “决定对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制” — _working translation:_ “decided to impose export controls on gallium- and germanium-related items”  
  > [商务部 海关总署公告2023年第23号 关于对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制的公告](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2023/art_c2ae3d2061e14e97ba608de1ed565f78.html), preamble
  > “本公告自2023年8月1日起正式实施” — _working translation:_ “This announcement takes effect on 1 August 2023”  
  > [商务部 海关总署公告2023年第23号 关于对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制的公告](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2023/art_c2ae3d2061e14e97ba608de1ed565f78.html), Article 8
  > the announcement is signed 商务部 海关总署 (MOFCOM and GAC) and dated 2023年7月3日 (3 July 2023). _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部 海关总署公告2023年第23号 关于对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制的公告](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2023/art_c2ae3d2061e14e97ba608de1ed565f78.html), signature block

- **MOFCOM issued Announcement No. 61 of 2025 on 9 October 2025, requiring foreign exporters to obtain a MOFCOM dual-use export licence for certain foreign-made items containing Chinese-origin rare-earth items, with parts effective from 1 December 2025 and the rest on publication.** `cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2025-61-issued`
  2025-10-09 · issued · China · `enacted`
  _Date:_ Parts 1(1) and 1(2) effective 1 December 2025; part 1(3) on publication (Article 8).
  _Limits:_ Records issuance and the licensing requirement in summary; the item list (attachment) was not retrieved. The announcement was suspended on 7 November 2025 (cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2025-70-suspension).
  > “必须获得中国商务部颁发的两用物项出口许可证件” — _working translation:_ “must obtain a dual-use item export licence issued by the Chinese Ministry of Commerce”  
  > [商务部公告2025年第61号 公布对境外相关稀土物项实施出口管制的决定](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2025/art_fbc5f7e23bff4bb184d5c49ff878f8ad.html), Article 1
  > parts 1(1) and 1(2) take effect on 1 December 2025 and part 1(3) on publication. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部公告2025年第61号 公布对境外相关稀土物项实施出口管制的决定](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2025/art_fbc5f7e23bff4bb184d5c49ff878f8ad.html), Article 8

#### What the text states

- **Announcement No. 23 of 2023 provides that listed gallium items (including gallium nitride and gallium arsenide) and germanium items (including germanium dioxide) may not be exported without a licence.** `cpo-ch-cn-ga-ge-2023-items`
  2023-08-01 · effective · China · `states_provision`
  _Limits:_ A licensing requirement, not a ban; it does not show licences refused or volumes exported.

  > “满足以下特性的物项，未经许可，不得出口” — _working translation:_ “Items with the following characteristics may not be exported without a licence”  
  > [商务部 海关总署公告2023年第23号 关于对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制的公告](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2023/art_c2ae3d2061e14e97ba608de1ed565f78.html), Article 1
  > the gallium list includes gallium nitride (氮化镓) and gallium arsenide (砷化镓); the germanium list (Article 1(2)) includes germanium dioxide (二氧化锗). _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部 海关总署公告2023年第23号 关于对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制的公告](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2023/art_c2ae3d2061e14e97ba608de1ed565f78.html), Article 1(1), items 2 and 5

- **MOFCOM Announcement No. 46 of 2024, dated 3 December 2024 and effective on publication, provides that in principle no licences will be granted for exports of gallium-, germanium-, antimony- and superhard-material-related dual-use items to the United States.** `cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2024-46-us`
  2024-12-03 · issued · China · `states_provision`
  _Date:_ Effective on publication.
  _Limits:_ States a licensing policy toward one destination; the text does not mention chips or AI. Paragraph 2, which carries this provision, was suspended from 9 November 2025 to 27 November 2026 by MOFCOM Announcement No. 72 of 2025.

  > “原则上不予许可镓、锗、锑、超硬材料相关两用物项对美国出口” — _working translation:_ “in principle, exports to the United States of gallium-, germanium-, antimony- and superhard-material-related dual-use items will not be licensed”  
  > [商务部公告2024年第46号 关于加强相关两用物项对美国出口管制的公告](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2024/art_3d5e990b43424e60828030f58a547b60.html), paragraph 2
  > the page gives 发文日期 2024年12月03日 (3 December 2024). _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部公告2024年第46号 关于加强相关两用物项对美国出口管制的公告](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2024/art_3d5e990b43424e60828030f58a547b60.html), metadata block
  > MOFCOM Announcement No. 72 of 2025 (dated 9 November 2025) suspends paragraph 2 of Announcement No. 46 of 2024 from that day until 27 November 2026. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部公告2025年第72号 公布调整实施商务部公告2024年第46号的决定](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2025/art_5c68985a6b1a46778e2e8dbff1bb1601.html), operative sentence · context

- **Announcement No. 61 of 2025, on export controls for overseas rare-earth-related items, provides that export licence applications for those items whose end use is research, development or production of logic chips of 14 nanometres or below or memory chips of 256 layers or above, related manufacturing and test equipment and materials, or research and development of AI with potential military use, are examined case by case.** `cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2025-61-chips-ai`
  2025-10-09 · issued · China · `states_provision`
  _Limits:_ A review standard for licence applications for the announcement’s rare-earth-related items, not a general export rule or a prohibition; it does not show any application decided. MOFCOM–GAC Announcement No. 70 of 2025 suspended Announcement No. 61 from 7 November 2025 to 10 November 2026 (cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2025-70-suspension).

  > “最终用途为研发、生产14纳米及以下逻辑芯片或者256层及以上存储芯片，以及制造上述制程半导体的生产设备、测试设备和材料，或者研发具有潜在军事用途的人工智能的出口申请，逐案审批。” — _working translation:_ “Export applications whose end use is the research, development or production of logic chips of 14 nm and below or memory chips of 256 layers and above, or of production equipment, test equipment and materials for manufacturing semiconductors at those processes, or the research and development of artificial intelligence with potential military uses, are examined and approved case by case.”  
  > [商务部公告2025年第61号 公布对境外相关稀土物项实施出口管制的决定](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2025/art_fbc5f7e23bff4bb184d5c49ff878f8ad.html), Article 4
  > the announcement’s title describes it as a decision to implement export controls on relevant overseas rare-earth items (公布对境外相关稀土物项实施出口管制的决定). _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部公告2025年第61号 公布对境外相关稀土物项实施出口管制的决定](https://aqygzj.mofcom.gov.cn/qdml/art/2025/art_fbc5f7e23bff4bb184d5c49ff878f8ad.html), title
  > MOFCOM–GAC Announcement No. 70 of 2025 (dated 7 November 2025) suspends MOFCOM Announcement No. 61 of 2025, among others, from that day until 10 November 2026. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [商务部、海关总署公告2025年第70号 公布暂停实施商务部、海关总署公告2025年第55号、56号、57号、58号及商务部公告2025年第61号、62号的决定](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2025/art_b1ec77dd3f0d4762952904df7cdaadec.html), operative sentence · context

- **Joint MOFCOM–GAC Announcement No. 70 of 2025, dated 7 November 2025, suspends MOFCOM Announcements No. 61 and No. 62 of 2025 (and joint Announcements Nos. 55 to 58 of 2025) from that day until 10 November 2026.** `cpo-ch-cn-mofcom-2025-70-suspension`
  2025-11-07 · issued · China · `states_provision`
  _Limits:_ A suspension for a stated period; Announcement No. 61 itself is not repealed. Whether the suspension was extended after the check date is not known. Announcements 55–58 and 62 are not recorded here.

  > [商务部、海关总署公告2025年第70号 公布暂停实施商务部、海关总署公告2025年第55号、56号、57号、58号及商务部公告2025年第61号、62号的决定](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2025/art_b1ec77dd3f0d4762952904df7cdaadec.html), operative sentence
  > “商务部公告2025年第61号、62号暂停实施” — _working translation:_ “MOFCOM Announcements No. 61 and 62 of 2025 are suspended”  
  > [商务部、海关总署公告2025年第70号 公布暂停实施商务部、海关总署公告2025年第55号、56号、57号、58号及商务部公告2025年第61号、62号的决定](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zwgk/zcfb/art/2025/art_b1ec77dd3f0d4762952904df7cdaadec.html), operative sentence
  > the announcement is issued by MOFCOM and the General Administration of Customs (商务部、海关总署公告2025年第70号) and also suspends their joint Announcements Nos. 55, 56, 57 and 58 of 2025. _(paraphrase)_

### NVIDIA H20 and H200 exports to China (2025–2026)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **NVIDIA stated in its first-quarter fiscal 2026 results release of 28 May 2025 that on 9 April 2025 the US government informed it that a licence is required for exports of its H20 products into the China market.** `cpo-ch-nvidia-h20-licence-requirement`
  2025-04-09 · announced · China · proposed predicate `export_licence_requirement_stated`
  _Date:_ Event date as NVIDIA states it (government notice to NVIDIA); NVIDIA’s statement is dated 28 May 2025. NVIDIA’s 8-K of April 2025 on the same notice could not be retrieved.
  _Limits:_ NVIDIA’s account of a government notification; no BIS rule or letter has been retrieved for it. It does not show whether licences were later applied for or granted.

  > “On April 9, 2025, NVIDIA was informed by the U.S. government that a license is required for exports of its H20 products into the China market.”  
  > [NVIDIA Announces Financial Results for First Quarter Fiscal 2026](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-announces-financial-results-for-first-quarter-fiscal-2026), paragraph 1

- **NVIDIA stated in its results release of 27 August 2025 that there were no H20 sales to China-based customers in the second quarter of fiscal 2026 and that its outlook for the third quarter assumed no H20 shipments to China.** `cpo-ch-nvidia-h20-no-china-sales-q2`
  2025-08-27 · announced · China · proposed predicate `export_licence_requirement_stated`
  _Date:_ Quarter ended 27 July 2025.
  _Limits:_ NVIDIA’s statement for one quarter; it does not show whether licences had been granted by then, and reported licensing arrangements of August 2025 rest on press reporting and are not recorded.
  > “There were no H20 sales to China-based customers in the second quarter.”  
  > [NVIDIA Announces Financial Results for Second Quarter Fiscal 2026](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-announces-financial-results-for-second-quarter-fiscal-2026), paragraph 1
  > “The company has not assumed any H20 shipments to China in the outlook.”  
  > [NVIDIA Announces Financial Results for Second Quarter Fiscal 2026](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-announces-financial-results-for-second-quarter-fiscal-2026), Outlook

#### Issue and status of texts

- **The Bureau of Industry and Security published the final rule "Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities" in the Federal Register of 15 January 2026, effective that day.** `cpo-ch-bis-license-policy-2026-published`
  2026-01-15 · published · US · `published`
  _Date:_ Effective 15 January 2026.
  _Limits:_ Records publication only; the rule’s content is in the following records.
  > “Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities AGENCY: Bureau of Industry and Security, Department of Commerce. ACTION: Final rule.”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1684, heading
  > “The effective date of this rule is January 15, 2026.”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1685, DATES

#### What the text states

- **The January 2026 rule changes the licence review policy for exports from the United States to end users in China or Macau of commodities with a TPP below 21,000 and total DRAM bandwidth below 6,500 GB/s, naming the NVIDIA H200 and AMD MI325X as examples, from a presumption of denial to case-by-case review, provided stated conditions and the applicant’s certifications are met.** `cpo-ch-bis-license-policy-2026-case-by-case`
  2026-01-15 · effective · China · `states_provision`
  _Limits:_ A licence review policy, not a licence: it does not show any licence granted or any shipment. Case-by-case review applies to exports from the United States to end users located in China or Macau, for chips commercially available in the United States at publication and with the required certifications; reexports and in-country transfers to Macau or Country Group D:5, and exports to entities headquartered (or with a parent headquartered) in Macau or D:5 wherever located, remain under a presumption of denial.
  > “changing it from a presumption of denial to a case-by-case review. The semiconductors covered by this rule are the Nvidia H200 and its equivalents, as well as less advanced chips”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1685, SUMMARY
  > “commodities with a TPP less than 21,000, and a ‘total DRAM bandwidth’ less than 6,500 GB/s (e.g., NVIDIA H200 or AMD MI325X)”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1685, section II (revision of § 742.6(b)(10))
  > “provided that (1) the semiconductors are commercially available in the United States at the time of publication of this rule”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1685, SUMMARY
  > “For exports to entities that are headquartered or have a parent company headquartered in Macau or a destination in Country Group D:5, including end-users located outside of destinations in Country Group D:5 or Macau, the licensing policy is a presumption of denial.”  
  > [Revision to License Review Policy for Advanced Computing Commodities (91 FR 1684)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-01-15/pdf/2026-00789.pdf), 91 FR 1685, section II

### Huawei Ascend roadmap (September 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Eric Xu stated, in Huawei’s published keynote transcript, at Huawei Connect on 18 September 2025 that over the next three years Huawei would work on three new Ascend chip series (950, 960 and 970), with the Ascend 950PR to be available in the first quarter of 2026, the 950DT in the fourth quarter of 2026, the 960 in the fourth quarter of 2027 and the 970 planned for the fourth quarter of 2028.** `cpo-ch-huawei-ascend-roadmap`
  2025-09-18 · announced · China · proposed predicate `announced_plan`
  _Date:_ Date line of the keynote transcript.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stated launch target: 2026-Q1 (stated by Huawei / 华为); stated launch target: 2026-Q4 (stated by Huawei / 华为); stated launch target: 2027-Q4 (stated by Huawei / 华为); stated launch target: 2028-Q4 (stated by Huawei / 华为)
  _Limits:_ A product roadmap with target dates; it does not show that any chip was produced or shipped, where it is manufactured, or in what volume. The speaker’s office at Huawei is not stated in the retrieved transcript and is not recorded.
  > “Over the next three years, we'll be working on three new series of Ascend chips: the Ascend 950 series, which includes the Ascend 950PR and the Ascend 950DT, the Ascend 960 series, and the Ascend 970 series.”  
  > [Groundbreaking SuperPoD Interconnect: Leading a New Paradigm for AI Infrastructure (Keynote Speech by Eric Xu at Huawei Connect 2025)](https://www.huawei.com/en/news/2025/9/hc-xu-keynote-speech), keynote, Ascend roadmap section
  > “This chip will be available in the first quarter of 2026.”  
  > [Groundbreaking SuperPoD Interconnect: Leading a New Paradigm for AI Infrastructure (Keynote Speech by Eric Xu at Huawei Connect 2025)](https://www.huawei.com/en/news/2025/9/hc-xu-keynote-speech), keynote, Ascend 950PR
  > “The Ascend 950DT chip will be available in the fourth quarter of 2026.”  
  > [Groundbreaking SuperPoD Interconnect: Leading a New Paradigm for AI Infrastructure (Keynote Speech by Eric Xu at Huawei Connect 2025)](https://www.huawei.com/en/news/2025/9/hc-xu-keynote-speech), keynote, Ascend 950DT
  > “The Ascend 960 chip will be available in the fourth quarter of 2027.”  
  > [Groundbreaking SuperPoD Interconnect: Leading a New Paradigm for AI Infrastructure (Keynote Speech by Eric Xu at Huawei Connect 2025)](https://www.huawei.com/en/news/2025/9/hc-xu-keynote-speech), keynote, Ascend 960
  > “The plan is to go to market with the Ascend 970 in the fourth quarter of 2028.”  
  > [Groundbreaking SuperPoD Interconnect: Leading a New Paradigm for AI Infrastructure (Keynote Speech by Eric Xu at Huawei Connect 2025)](https://www.huawei.com/en/news/2025/9/hc-xu-keynote-speech), keynote, Ascend 970

### HUMAIN with NVIDIA and AMD (May 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **AMD and HUMAIN announced on 13 May 2025 an agreement under which the parties will invest up to $10 billion to deploy 500 megawatts of AI compute capacity over five years, with HUMAIN delivering the data-centre, power and fibre infrastructure and AMD providing its AI compute portfolio and ROCm software.** `cpo-ch-humain-amd`
  2025-05-13 · announced · Gulf · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 500 MW (as worded: '500 megawatts of AI compute capacity'; the subheading says 'up to 500 megawatts of AI infrastructure') (measures: not stated); amount up to 10,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stage: announced; stated target operation: 2026 (stated by Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (AMD))
  _Limits:_ The $10 billion is stated for "the parties" together and is not attributed to either. A five-year plan; AMD also states that initial deployments are underway and that the collaboration is on track for multi-exaflop capacity by early 2026, which is a target, not a result.

  > “the parties will invest up to $10B to deploy 500 megawatts of AI compute capacity over the next five years.”  
  > [AMD and HUMAIN Form Strategic, $10B Collaboration to Advance Global AI](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1250/amd-and-humain-form-strategic-10b-collaboration-to-advance-global-ai), body, paragraph 2
  > “HUMAIN will oversee end-to-end delivery, including hyperscale data center, sustainable power systems, and global fiber interconnects, and AMD will provide the full spectrum of the AMD AI compute portfolio and the AMD ROCm™ open software ecosystem.”  
  > [AMD and HUMAIN Form Strategic, $10B Collaboration to Advance Global AI](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1250/amd-and-humain-form-strategic-10b-collaboration-to-advance-global-ai), body, paragraph 2
  > “on track to activate multi-exaflop capacity by early 2026”  
  > [AMD and HUMAIN Form Strategic, $10B Collaboration to Advance Global AI](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1250/amd-and-humain-form-strategic-10b-collaboration-to-advance-global-ai), body, paragraph 5

- **HUMAIN and NVIDIA announced on 13 May 2025, in a release on NVIDIA's newsroom, that HUMAIN was making an investment to build AI factories in Saudi Arabia with a projected capacity of up to 500 megawatts powered by several hundred thousand NVIDIA GPUs over five years, the first phase being an 18,000-chip NVIDIA GB300 Grace Blackwell AI supercomputer.** `cpo-ch-humain-nvidia`
  2025-05-13 · announced · Gulf · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity up to 500 MW (as worded: 'projected capacity of up to 500 megawatts') (measures: not stated); stage: announced; supply: GPUs, several hundred thousand NVIDIA GPUs over five years; first phase 18,000 GB300
  _Limits:_ A projected capacity over five years and an announced first phase; it does not show GPUs shipped, an export licence granted, or a contract price. "Several hundred thousand" is the release’s wording, not a count.
  > HUMAIN is making a major investment to build AI factories in the Kingdom of Saudi Arabia; the release is headed "HUMAIN and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership". _(paraphrase)_  
  > [HUMAIN and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Build AI Factories of the Future in Saudi Arabia](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/humain-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-build-ai-factories-of-the-future-in-saudi-arabia), body, paragraph 1
  > “with a projected capacity of up to 500 megawatts powered by several hundred thousand of NVIDIA’s most advanced GPUs over the next five years. The first phase of deployment will be an 18,000 NVIDIA GB300 Grace Blackwell AI supercomputer”  
  > [HUMAIN and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Build AI Factories of the Future in Saudi Arabia](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/humain-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-build-ai-factories-of-the-future-in-saudi-arabia), body, paragraph 1

### NVIDIA manufacturing in the United States (April 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **NVIDIA stated on 14 April 2025 that NVIDIA Blackwell chips had started production at TSMC’s chip plants in Phoenix, Arizona.** `cpo-ch-nvidia-blackwell-tsmc-arizona`
  2025-04-14 · stated operating · US · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ Dated by NVIDIA’s statement; the date production started is not given.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stage: operational; supply: foundry, not stated
  _Limits:_ NVIDIA’s statement that production had started; it gives no volume, no yield and no statement by TSMC, and does not show that packaged chips were completed in the United States (the post names Amkor and SPIL for packaging and testing in Arizona as partners).

  > “NVIDIA Blackwell chips have started production at TSMC’s chip plants in Phoenix, Arizona.”  
  > [NVIDIA to Manufacture American-Made AI Supercomputers in US for First Time](https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-manufacture-american-made-ai-supercomputers-us/), paragraph 3

- **NVIDIA stated on 14 April 2025 that within the next four years it plans to produce up to half a trillion dollars of AI infrastructure in the United States through partnerships with TSMC, Foxconn, Wistron, Amkor and SPIL.** `cpo-ch-nvidia-us-production-plan`
  2025-04-14 · announced · US · `plans_infrastructure_spend`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount up to 500,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stage: planned
  _Limits:_ A four-year plan stated by NVIDIA, worded as "up to half a trillion dollars" of AI infrastructure to be produced; it is not a spending commitment by NVIDIA alone, an investment amount or a result, and the source does not say how the value is measured.
  > “Within the next four years, NVIDIA plans to produce up to half a trillion dollars of AI infrastructure in the United States”  
  > [NVIDIA to Manufacture American-Made AI Supercomputers in US for First Time](https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-manufacture-american-made-ai-supercomputers-us/), paragraph 5
  > through partnerships with TSMC, Foxconn, Wistron, Amkor and SPIL. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [NVIDIA to Manufacture American-Made AI Supercomputers in US for First Time](https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-manufacture-american-made-ai-supercomputers-us/), paragraph 5

### OpenAI and AMD: 6 GW agreement and warrant (October 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **The joint AMD–OpenAI release of 6 October 2025 stated that AMD had issued OpenAI a warrant for up to 160 million shares of AMD common stock, vesting as milestones are achieved: the first tranche with the initial 1 gigawatt deployment, further tranches as purchases scale up to 6 gigawatts, and vesting further tied to AMD share-price targets and to OpenAI technical and commercial milestones.** `cpo-ch-amd-openai-warrant`
  2025-10-06 · announced · US · proposed predicate `granted_equity_right`
  _Date:_ The release says the warrant "has" been issued; the issue date is not separately stated.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity up to 160000000 shares of AMD common stock (as worded: 'up to 160 million shares') (measures: shares); stage: announced
  _Limits:_ Records the warrant as AMD describes it. It does not show that any tranche has vested or been exercised, the exercise price, or the share-price targets, which the release does not state.

  > “AMD has issued OpenAI a warrant for up to 160 million shares of AMD common stock, structured to vest as specific milestones are achieved.”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), body, paragraph 4
  > “The first tranche vests with the initial 1 gigawatt deployment, with additional tranches vesting as purchases scale up to 6 gigawatts.”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), body, paragraph 4
  > “Vesting is further tied to AMD achieving certain share-price targets and to OpenAI achieving the technical and commercial milestones”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), body, paragraph 4

- **AMD and OpenAI announced on 6 October 2025, in a joint release, a definitive agreement under which OpenAI is to deploy 6 gigawatts of AMD Instinct GPUs across multiple generations, beginning with a 1 gigawatt deployment of MI450 GPUs set to start in the second half of 2026.** `cpo-ch-openai-amd-agreement`
  2025-10-06 · announced · US · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ Date of the AMD release; the release does not give a separate signature date.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 6 GW (as worded: '6 gigawatts of AMD GPUs') (measures: not stated); stage: contracted; supply: GPUs, 6 gigawatts of AMD Instinct GPUs, first 1 gigawatt MI450; stated delivery target: 2026-H2 (stated by Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (AMD))
  _Limits:_ The release calls the agreement definitive; the agreement text is not public here. A planned deployment is not a delivery: no GPU shipment, deployment or price is shown. The 6 GW figure is not converted into chips or revenue.
  > “OpenAI to deploy 6 gigawatts of AMD GPUs based on a multi-year, multi-generation agreement”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), News Highlights, bullet 1
  > “The first 1 gigawatt deployment of AMD Instinct MI450 GPUs is set to begin in the second half of 2026.”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), body, paragraph 1
  > “Under this definitive agreement, OpenAI will work with AMD as a core strategic compute partner”  
  > [AMD and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 6 Gigawatts of AMD GPUs](https://ir.amd.com/news-events/press-releases/detail/1260/amd-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-6-gigawatts-of-amd-gpus), body, paragraph 3

### OpenAI and Broadcom: 10 GW custom accelerators (October 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **The joint OpenAI–Broadcom release of 13 October 2025 announced a collaboration for 10 gigawatts of custom AI accelerators designed by OpenAI and developed and deployed with Broadcom, with deployment of racks targeted to start in the second half of 2026 and to be completed by the end of 2029.** `cpo-ch-openai-broadcom-collaboration`
  2025-10-13 · announced · US · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ Dateline of the release.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity 10 GW (as worded: '10 gigawatts of custom AI accelerators') (measures: not stated); stage: announced; supply: accelerators, 10 gigawatts of OpenAI-designed AI accelerators, in racks with Broadcom networking; stated delivery target: 2026-H2 (stated by Broadcom); stated delivery target: 2029-12 (stated by Broadcom)
  _Limits:_ An announced collaboration with target dates; no rack has been shown delivered or deployed, and no price is stated. The 10 GW figure is not converted to chips or dollars. It is distinct from the Google–Broadcom TPU arrangements with Anthropic already in the dataset.

  > “today announced a collaboration for 10 gigawatts of custom AI accelerators.”  
  > [OpenAI and Broadcom announce strategic collaboration to deploy 10 gigawatts of OpenAI-designed AI accelerators](https://mfn.se/all/a/broadcom/openai-and-broadcom-announce-strategic-collaboration-to-deploy-10-gigawatts-of-openai-designed-ai-accelerators-7f50bad2), body, paragraph 1 (after dateline)

  > [OpenAI and Broadcom announce strategic collaboration to deploy 10 gigawatts of OpenAI-designed AI accelerators](https://mfn.se/all/a/broadcom/openai-and-broadcom-announce-strategic-collaboration-to-deploy-10-gigawatts-of-openai-designed-ai-accelerators-7f50bad2), body, paragraph 1 (after dateline)
  > “Broadcom to deploy racks of AI accelerator and network systems targeted to start in the second half of 2026, to complete by end of 2029”  
  > [OpenAI and Broadcom announce strategic collaboration to deploy 10 gigawatts of OpenAI-designed AI accelerators](https://mfn.se/all/a/broadcom/openai-and-broadcom-announce-strategic-collaboration-to-deploy-10-gigawatts-of-openai-designed-ai-accelerators-7f50bad2), highlights, bullet 2

  2025-10-13 · announced · US · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`

### OpenAI and NVIDIA: 10 GW letter of intent (September 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **The joint OpenAI–NVIDIA release of 22 September 2025 stated that NVIDIA intended to invest up to $100 billion in OpenAI progressively as each gigawatt of NVIDIA systems is deployed.** `cpo-ch-nvidia-openai-investment-intent`
  2025-09-22 · announced · US · `investment_announced`
  _Date:_ Date of the NVIDIA release.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount up to 100,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stage: letter_of_intent
  _Limits:_ A stated intention under a letter of intent, conditional on deployment ("progressively as each gigawatt is deployed"). It records no invested amount, no closing and no payment, and does not show the size of any single tranche. Reports of 2026 that the arrangement changed rest on press reporting and are not recorded here (see openQuestions).

  > “NVIDIA intends to invest up to $100 billion in OpenAI progressively as each gigawatt is deployed.”  
  > [OpenAI and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 10 Gigawatts of NVIDIA Systems](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/openai-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-10gw-of-nvidia-systems), highlights, bullet 2

- **OpenAI and NVIDIA announced on 22 September 2025, in a joint release on NVIDIA's newsroom, a letter of intent for a partnership to deploy at least 10 gigawatts of NVIDIA systems for OpenAI’s AI infrastructure, with the first gigawatt targeted for the second half of 2026 on the NVIDIA Vera Rubin platform.** `cpo-ch-openai-nvidia-loi`
  2025-09-22 · announced · US · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ Date of the NVIDIA release.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ quantity at least 10 GW (as worded: 'at least 10 gigawatts of NVIDIA systems') (measures: not stated); stage: letter_of_intent; supply: systems, at least 10 gigawatts of NVIDIA systems; stated delivery target: 2026-H2 (stated by NVIDIA)
  _Limits:_ A letter of intent, not a definitive agreement: the release says the companies looked forward to finalising the details "in the coming weeks". It does not show that a definitive agreement was signed, that any system was delivered or deployed, or what the systems cost. The gigawatt figure is not converted to chips or dollars here; the release’s phrase "millions of GPUs" is a description, not a count. Later status of the letter is an open question.
  > “OpenAI and NVIDIA today announced a letter of intent for a landmark strategic partnership to deploy at least 10 gigawatts of NVIDIA systems”  
  > [OpenAI and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 10 Gigawatts of NVIDIA Systems](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/openai-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-10gw-of-nvidia-systems), body, paragraph 1
  > “The first gigawatt of NVIDIA systems will be deployed in the second half of 2026 on the NVIDIA Vera Rubin platform.”  
  > [OpenAI and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 10 Gigawatts of NVIDIA Systems](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/openai-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-10gw-of-nvidia-systems), highlights, bullet 3
  > “look forward to finalizing the details of this new phase of strategic partnership in the coming weeks”  
  > [OpenAI and NVIDIA Announce Strategic Partnership to Deploy 10 Gigawatts of NVIDIA Systems](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/openai-and-nvidia-announce-strategic-partnership-to-deploy-10gw-of-nvidia-systems), body, last paragraph before "About OpenAI"

### Stargate memory letters of intent: Samsung and SK hynix (October 2025)

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **Samsung's release of 1 October 2025 stated that OpenAI and four Samsung companies had announced a letter of intent under which Samsung Electronics will work with OpenAI as a strategic memory partner to supply advanced semiconductor solutions for OpenAI’s Stargate initiative.** `cpo-ch-samsung-openai-loi`
  2025-10-01 · announced · Other · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ Dateline of the Samsung release.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stage: letter_of_intent; supply: memory, not stated (demand projection: 'up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month', attributed to OpenAI)
  _Limits:_ A letter of intent, not a supply contract: no volume, price or delivery from Samsung is stated. The release’s figure of up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month is OpenAI’s projected memory demand, not a quantity Samsung undertakes to supply; it says only that Samsung "will contribute toward meeting this need". The LOI also covers Samsung SDS, Samsung C&T and Samsung Heavy Industries, not recorded here.

  > the parties announcing the LOI are OpenAI, Samsung Electronics, Samsung SDS, Samsung C&T and Samsung Heavy Industries. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Samsung and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Accelerate Advancements in Global AI Infrastructure](https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-accelerate-advancements-in-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 1
  > “today announced a letter of intent (LOI) for their strategic partnership”  
  > [Samsung and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Accelerate Advancements in Global AI Infrastructure](https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-accelerate-advancements-in-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 1
  > “Samsung Electronics will work with OpenAI as a strategic memory partner to supply advanced semiconductor solutions for OpenAI’s global Stargate initiative. With OpenAI’s memory demand projected to reach up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month”  
  > [Samsung and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Accelerate Advancements in Global AI Infrastructure](https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-accelerate-advancements-in-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 3
  > the release says Samsung will contribute toward meeting OpenAI's projected memory need with its DRAM solutions. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Samsung and OpenAI Announce Strategic Partnership to Accelerate Advancements in Global AI Infrastructure](https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-openai-announce-strategic-partnership-to-accelerate-advancements-in-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 3 · context

- **SK Group announced on 1 October 2025 that SK hynix had signed a letter of intent with OpenAI to supply High Bandwidth Memory for Stargate data centres and would establish a production system able to meet OpenAI’s HBM demand of up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month.** `cpo-ch-skhynix-openai-loi`
  2025-10-01 · signed · Other · proposed predicate `supply_agreement`
  _Date:_ The release says the LOI was signed at the 1 October 2025 meeting in Seoul.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stage: letter_of_intent; supply: memory, HBM; production system for demand of 'up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month' (plan)
  _Limits:_ A letter of intent; SK’s statement of a production system to be established is a plan, not capacity in place, and no supply volume, price or delivery is shown. SK’s further statement that this demand is "more than double the current HBM industry capacity" is SK’s comparison and is not checked here. The SK Telecom MOU on a Korean data centre belongs to the data-centre strand.
  > “SK hynix signed a Letter of Intent (LOI) with OpenAI to supply High Bandwidth Memory (HBM), for the construction of global AI data centers under the Stargate project.”  
  > [SK Group Partners with OpenAI to Advance Global AI Infrastructure](https://eng.sk.com/news/sk-group-partners-with-openai-to-advance-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 2
  > “SK hynix will establish a production system capable of promptly meeting OpenAI’s HBM demand for up to 900,000 DRAM wafers per month. It is more than double the current HBM industry capacity”  
  > [SK Group Partners with OpenAI to Advance Global AI Infrastructure](https://eng.sk.com/news/sk-group-partners-with-openai-to-advance-global-ai-infrastructure), body, paragraph 2

### TSMC Arizona: CHIPS award and investment

#### Announcements, plans and agreements

- **President Biden’s statement of 8 April 2024 said the Department of Commerce was announcing a preliminary agreement with TSMC to support the construction of leading-edge semiconductor manufacturing facilities in the United States, and that TSMC would also build a third chip factory in Phoenix, increasing its total investment in Arizona to $65 billion.** `cpo-ch-tsmc-chips-preliminary`
  2024-04-08 · announced · US · proposed predicate `government_award`
  _Date:_ Date of the presidential statement; NIST later dates the preliminary memorandum of terms to an announcement of 8 April 2024.
  _Figures and stated dates:_ stage: preliminary
  _Limits:_ A preliminary agreement; the total award amount is not stated in this statement (it mentions $50 million of CHIPS funding for workforce training). The $65 billion is TSMC’s stated total Arizona investment, not the award. The statement names Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company; TSMC Arizona is named as awardee in the final award release. The statement does not mention AI; the AI reference appears in the final award release (cpo-ch-tsmc-chips-final-award).

  > “Department of Commerce announcing a preliminary agreement with Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company (TSMC)”  
  > [Statement on the Preliminary Funding Agreement Between the Department of Commerce and Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company Under the CHIPS and Science Act (DCPD-202400273)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400273/html/DCPD-202400273.htm), paragraph 2
  > the preliminary agreement is to support the construction of leading-edge semiconductor manufacturing facilities in the United States. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Statement on the Preliminary Funding Agreement Between the Department of Commerce and Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company Under the CHIPS and Science Act (DCPD-202400273)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400273/html/DCPD-202400273.htm), paragraph 2
  > “TSMC will also build a third chip factory in Phoenix, increasing its total investment in Arizona to $65 billion”  
  > [Statement on the Preliminary Funding Agreement Between the Department of Commerce and Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company Under the CHIPS and Science Act (DCPD-202400273)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400273/html/DCPD-202400273.htm), paragraph 2
  > the agreement also dedicates $50 million of CHIPS funding to training and developing the local workforce. _(paraphrase)_  
  > [Statement on the Preliminary Funding Agreement Between the Department of Commerce and Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company Under the CHIPS and Science Act (DCPD-202400273)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400273/html/DCPD-202400273.htm), paragraph 2 · context
  > “previously signed preliminary memorandum of terms, announced on April 8, 2024”  
  > [Biden-Harris Administration Announces CHIPS Incentives Award with TSMC Arizona to Secure U.S. Leadership in Advanced Semiconductor Technology](https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2024/11/biden-harris-administration-announces-chips-incentives-award-tsmc-arizona), paragraph 1

- **NIST announced on 15 November 2024 that the Department of Commerce had awarded TSMC Arizona up to $6.6 billion in direct CHIPS Incentives funding, to be disbursed on completion of project milestones, plus up to $5 billion of proposed loans, supporting a planned investment of more than $65 billion in three Phoenix fabs; the release says the chips will power artificial intelligence among other industries.** `cpo-ch-tsmc-chips-final-award`
  2024-11-15 · announced · US · proposed predicate `government_award`
  _Date:_ Date of the NIST release announcing the final award; the award agreement itself was not retrieved (integrator’s correction before the audit: basis was "approval").
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount up to 6,600,000,000 USD (transaction); stage: final
  _Limits:_ An award, not a disbursement: funds are paid against milestones, and the loans are "proposed". The AI reference describes the expected uses of chips, not a condition of the award. The served page states it was updated on 6 October 2025.

  > “awarded TSMC Arizona Corporation (TSMC Arizona), a subsidiary of Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company Limited (TSMC), up to $6.6 billion in direct funding”  
  > [Biden-Harris Administration Announces CHIPS Incentives Award with TSMC Arizona to Secure U.S. Leadership in Advanced Semiconductor Technology](https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2024/11/biden-harris-administration-announces-chips-incentives-award-tsmc-arizona), paragraph 1

  2025-03-03 · announced · US · `plans_infrastructure_spend`
  _Figures and stated dates:_ amount 100,000,000,000 USD (transaction); stage: announced

Announced investment only: no construction, spending or production is shown, and the facilities are described differently by the speakers (TSMC’s own breakdown is not recorded here). The $165 billion total is the President’s figure.

### US export controls on advanced computing (2023–2026)

#### Issue and status of texts

- **The Bureau of Industry and Security published the interim final rule "Implementation of Additional Export Controls: Certain Advanced Computing Items; Supercomputer and Semiconductor End Use; Updates and Corrections" in the Federal Register of 25 October 2023, effective 17 November 2023, revising the advanced computing controls of 7 October 2022.** `cpo-ch-bis-acr-2023-published`
  2023-10-25 · published · US · `published`
  _Date:_ Effective 17 November 2023 (one amendatory instruction effective to 1 January 2026); comments due 18 December 2023.
  _Limits:_ Records publication and stated dates. The 7 October 2022 rule it revises is earlier than this pack’s period and is not recorded. A companion rule on semiconductor manufacturing items published the same day is not recorded (omission).

  > “Federal Register / Vol. 88, No. 205 / Wednesday, October 25, 2023 / Rules and Regulations”  
  > [Implementation of Additional Export Controls: Certain Advanced Computing Items; Supercomputer and Semiconductor End Use; Updates and Corrections (88 FR 73458)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2023-10-25/pdf/2023-23055.pdf), 88 FR 73458, masthead
  > “This rule is effective November … 17, 2023, except for amendatory instruction 11”  
  > [Implementation of Additional Export Controls: Certain Advanced Computing Items; Supercomputer and Semiconductor End Use; Updates and Corrections (88 FR 73458)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2023-10-25/pdf/2023-23055.pdf), 88 FR 73458, DATES
  > “This Advanced Computing/Supercomputing IFR (AC/S IFR) addresses comments received in response to only the part of the October 7 IFR that controls advanced computing ICs”  
  > [Implementation of Additional Export Controls: Certain Advanced Computing Items; Supercomputer and Semiconductor End Use; Updates and Corrections (88 FR 73458)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2023-10-25/pdf/2023-23055.pdf), 88 FR 73458, SUMMARY

- **The Bureau of Industry and Security published the interim final rule "Foreign-Produced Direct Product Rule Additions, and Refinements to Controls for Advanced Computing and Semiconductor Manufacturing Items" in the Federal Register of 5 December 2024, effective 2 December 2024.** `cpo-ch-bis-fdp-hbm-2024-published`
  2024-12-05 · published · US · `published`
  _Date:_ Effective 2 December 2024, before publication; compliance dates for several instructions (including the HBM controls) 31 December 2024.
  _Limits:_ Records publication and the stated effective date. The rule’s semiconductor-manufacturing-equipment and foreign direct product provisions are not recorded; the concurrent Entity List rule is not recorded.

  > “Federal Register / Vol. 89, No. 234 / Thursday, December 5, 2024 / Rules and Regulations”  
  > [Foreign-Produced Direct Product Rule Additions, and Refinements to Controls for Advanced Computing and Semiconductor Manufacturing Items (89 FR 96790)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2024-12-05/pdf/2024-28270.pdf), 89 FR 96790, masthead
  > “Effective date: This rule is effective December 2, 2024.”
